# Naming and layout migration (2026-10)

On 2026-10-26 the configuration moved to the Doom-templated layout (see [module-system.md](../../design/module-system.md)): every module became a directory `lisp/CATEGORY/NAME/` whose files are features named by their path, the core became `lisp/core/aaron-*.el`, and every `my/` symbol was renamed. No compatibility alias was kept. This record is the lookup table from old names to new ones; it is not updated afterwards.

Rules: an ordinary symbol `my/X` became `+X`; a command became `+DOMAIN/ACTION`, where DOMAIN is the domain word its name started with (`my/git-board` → `+git/board`) or else the module's name (`my/close-window` → `+windows/close-window`); symbols of the core, `early-init.el`, `init.el` and `bootstrap.el` became `aaron-` / `aaron/`; the `my/noema-…` names defined in Noema's `noema-xwidget-keys.el` became `noema-…`. `etc/config-store.el`, M-x history (amx, savehist), transient history, `bin/emacs-app`, the Raycast extension and Noema were migrated in the same change.
